# Tasks

This document is the working task queue for the repository.

Its purpose is to tell a human or AI agent what should be worked on next, what is currently active, what is blocked, and what completion looks like.

This file should be kept current.

---

## How to use this file

1. Start with the **Active Task** section.
2. If there is no active task, pick the top unblocked item from **Priority Queue**.
3. Before implementation, confirm:
   - scope
   - affected modules/layers
   - expected tests
   - documentation impact
4. After completion:
   - **update sub-task status in this file immediately** (mark the sub-task done, e.g. `[x]` or move to completed list) so other IDEs and agents know where to take over
   - move the task to Completed when the whole phase/task is done
   - update follow-ups if needed
   - update `docs/STATE.md` if current reality changed

Do not start multiple unrelated tasks at once unless explicitly instructed.

---

**Phase 13 v3.X-next.3.0 — DEC-031 v3.X-next.3 planning checkpoint ratified — done 2026-05-22 (predecessor commit to this v3.X-next.3 implementation beat immediately above; docs-only sweep on top of v3.X-next.6's operator-recovery affordance commit immediately below; the v3.X-next.3 implementation scope reframes to "wire the dormant Q3=(c) `call_graph_resolver` hook" instead). **Refined diagnosis (load-bearing for Q1) — two distinct invoke-gap scenarios + one explicitly-rejected phantom:** Plan-mode pre-read of [`androscan/analysis/slicing.py:1668-1683`](androscan/analysis/slicing.py) refined the v3.X-next.3 stub's framing into: **(A) cross-module unresolvable** — `owner not in classes_by_smali` AND no resolver wired → silent `continue` at line 1683; **(B) inherited-from-app-superclass** — `owner in classes_by_smali` but the specific `target_sig` isn't a method body declared on that class (it's inherited from an app-code parent); today the slicer happily emits a CallSite pointing at a non-existent body and the FE renders a phantom node that looks like a terminal even though it should keep walking. **Crucially:** when a CallSite IS built in either branch, `instruction_index` + `in_branch_of` + `branch_label` come from the slicer's local `enumerate` loop + `dominance.get(idx)` — both computed locally per the v3.X-next.1 lockset — so resolver-recovered CallSites carry **full CFG metadata, byte-equal-shape to a same-module one** and flow through `executionFlowGraphV3.ts::emitInvokeEdges` with zero special handling. **Phantom hypothesis explicitly rejected:** "framework-inherited method noise" (e.g. `MainActivity.getResources()` inherited from `Landroid/app/Activity;`) is NOT a real gap — the smali assembler resolves invoke-virtual to the *declaring* class at compile time per Dalvik bytecode convention, so the smali `invoke-*` line carries `Landroid/app/Activity;->getResources()` directly + the `FRAMEWORK_CLASS_PREFIXES` filter at `slicing.py:1660` catches it before any resolution work runs. Pivoting v3.X-next.3 to "drop inherited-framework-noise" would have been chasing a non-existent bug. **Original Q5=(b)/(c) `synthesise-from-neighbors()` framing also explicitly rejected** (carried forward verbatim from the v3.X-next.2.0 diminishing-returns analysis): synthesised orphan-leaf CallSites would lack `instruction_index` + dominance entirely (those are properties of a calling-method's instruction stream, not properties of the call graph's adjacency), so they'd render as the flat horizontal fan DEC-031's diagnosis was meant to fix. **Locked Q-matrix (one-line summary; full table + rationale in DEC-031 v3.X-next.3.0 follow-up; zero operator divergence — operator picked scope `(e)` at the plan-mode 2-question AskQuestion + commit-shape `(i)` two-commit split):** **Q1=(e)** combined (a) inherited-from-app-superclass + (b) cross-module via shared resolver path — unifies both real gap classes under one resolver wire-up; the framework-inherited phantom + the `neighbors()` synthesis stay rejected. **Q2=(a)** new public `call_graph.lookup_method_ref(decompile_cache_dir, smali_id) -> Optional[MethodRef]` — thin wrapper over private `_lookup_node` with per-call connection; memoization lives in `trace_behavior`, not `call_graph`, so the wrapper stays stateless + mirrors the `neighbors()` / `paths()` public-API shape already established in `call_graph.py`. **Q3=(a)** per-trace dict-memoization in a closure captured by `_walk_closure` — lifetime = one anchor build; same `target_sig` recurs across multiple caller bodies in any non-trivial closure so per-anchor cache lifetime is the right shape without complicating the public-API surface. **Q4=(a)** INFO-log resolver stats on `trace_behavior` completion: `{hits_inherited, hits_cross_module, misses, total}` — no `BehaviorAnchor` payload changes, no FE surface area; operator can `grep` the log after re-tracing a real app to confirm whether the (a)/(b) paths actually fired. **Q5=(a)** Same-module branch in `extract_call_sites` checks `target_sig in cls.method_sigs`; on miss, defer to resolver (treats inherited-from-app-superclass identically to cross-module); framework filter at line 1660 stays untouched as the outer gate; `cls.method_sigs` is a frozenset computed once per `cls` at function entry for O(1) per-invoke cost. **Commit-shape decision (third Ask):** operator picked **(i) two-commit split** — this v3.X-next.3.0 docs-only ratification commit first, then the v3.X-next.3 implementation. Mirrors v3.X-next.1.0 → v3.X-next.1 + v3.X-next.2.0 → v3.X-next.2 cadence per DEC-031 Q1=(b′); keeps the planning record committed before any code lands. **Visual-safety guarantees (carried into the v3.X-next.3 implementation contract):** **Zero FE changes** — recovered CallSites carry full CFG metadata + flow through the existing `emitInvokeEdges` path with byte-equal data shape. **vitest 29/29 should pass unchanged** — the existing emitter tests pin shape on linear / branchy / nested-locals and recovered CallSites match that shape exactly. **Edges can only appear, never disappear or restyle** — worst-case visual regression on a fresh re-trace = a new edge surfaces where there wasn't one before (that's the intended improvement). **Cached traces are unaffected until re-traced** — existing WeakBank flowchart won't move until the operator re-traces. **No new decision-tier locks** — the Q1-Q5 are implementation-detail (resolver public-API shape + memoization scope + stats observability + same-module branch unification), so DEC-031 status stays Active without sub-section additions and the locks live verbatim in the v3.X-next.3 row's deliverable column under § Phase 13 v3.X-next sub-step backlog. **Sub-step backlog status update (this commit):** new v3.X-next.3.0 row added marked `[x]` above the existing v3.X-next.3 stub row; **Phase 13 v3.X-next.6 — Restart + Clear-overlay buttons on `DynamicTraceRunControl` — one-click operator-recovery affordances for "I mis-clicked in the Android app mid-trace" — done 2026-05-22 (predecessor commit to this v3.X-next.3.0 docs-only ratification immediately above; FE-only follow-up to v3.X-next.2's dynamic-overlay surface in production; closes the operator-recovery gap surfaced 2026-05-21 during dogfood; policy = (c) Both buttons — Restart as primary recovery + Clear-overlay as tertiary post-stop wipe).** Two-file FE change on top of v3.X-next.2's promoted-to-production dynamic-overlay surface. **Background.** v3.X-next.2's `Stop` button deliberately preserves runtime overlays (`firedMethods` / `liveValues` / `summaries` / `hookFailed`) for post-mortem inspection — operator stops the Frida session and continues to see what fired. That posture left no in-UI recovery path for "I mis-clicked in the Android app and want to redo the trace": pre-v3.X-next.6 the only options were anchor-switch (heavy-handed; fires the `anchorKey` reset useEffect that also clears Inspector selection), app-switch (heavier), or full page reload. **Operator pick at design Ask:** **(c) Both buttons** over the leaner (a) strict-clear-only and (b) restart-only alternatives. Restart serves the "I mis-clicked, let me redo" workflow with a single click (`stop` if running, then `start(prev)`); Clear serves the "I'm done inspecting, give me a blank overlay" workflow. **Code changes (2 files touched, ~+70 LOC + doc-comment).** **(1) `androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/LabTraceMode.tsx`** (~+45 LOC) — `DynamicTraceRunControl` destructure extended `{ state, start, stop }` → `{ state, start, stop, reset }` to pull the pre-existing `useDynamicTrace.reset()` callback into scope; two new `useCallback` handlers `onRestart` (if `running`, `await stop()` then `void start({entry, hops})` — the hook's `start()` already wipes state to `INITIAL_DYNAMIC_TRACE_STATE` before re-arming so no explicit `reset()` is needed between stop + start) + `onClear` (just `reset()`); new visibility flags `hasRuntimeState = firedMethods.size > 0 || liveValues.size > 0 || summaries.size > 0 || hookFailed.size > 0` + `showRestart = (isRunning || hasRuntimeState) && !isBusy` + `showClear = hasRuntimeState && !isRunning && !isBusy`; two new conditional `<button>` elements rendered between the existing Run/Stop button and the status copy — Restart with warn-orange-on-hover styling + Clear with muted-on-default + text-on-hover styling. Detailed doc-comment block above the new logic explains the visibility matrix + the design rationale + the `start()`-wipes-state observation that makes the explicit `reset()` between `stop` + `start` unnecessary in the Restart handler. **(2) `androscan/web/frontend/src/App.css`** (~+25 LOC) — two new rules `.lab-dynamic-trace-button-restart` (border-default + text-default base; `--warn` border + color on hover; matches the `.lab-dynamic-trace-status-warn` warning hue family) + `.lab-dynamic-trace-button-clear` (border-default + `--muted` base; `--text` border + color on hover; tertiary-action restraint) inserted between the existing `.lab-dynamic-trace-button-running` and `.lab-dynamic-trace-status` rules. **Visibility matrix (final):** **idle / no session ever** → no extra buttons (Run/Stop control stays uncluttered). **starting / stopping** → no extra buttons (`isBusy` gate prevents queuing an action on top of an in-flight transition). **running, no events yet** → Restart visible only (Clear hidden — clearing while events stream in would just re-populate). **running, events fired** → Restart visible only. **stopped, has runtime state** → BOTH Restart + Clear visible (the post-mortem cleanup workflow). **stopped, no events** → no extra buttons. **error / disconnected with events** → BOTH visible (same as stopped). **error / disconnected, no events** → no extra buttons. **Verification.** **pytest 1330 → 1330 passed** (zero regressions; no BE touched); **vitest 29/29 passed** (no new tests — pure UI affordance, no logic surface; the existing `useDynamicTrace.reset` / `.start` / `.stop` semantics they leverage are already covered by the v3.X-next.2 emitter tests + the v3.X-next.1 BE tests + the FE infra Phase 13 v1 / 13.8 substrate); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (CSS 132.52 → 132.87 KB / **+0.35 KB raw** for the two new button rules + doc comment; JS 1507.38 → 1508.28 KB / **+0.90 KB raw** for the callbacks + button JSX + visibility flags + doc comment). Zero lint impact across both touched files. **Edge cases handled.** (1) `Restart` during `running` calls `await stop()` first which sends DELETE to the BE — clean session teardown, not a leaked Frida session relying on the BE's session-timeout GC. (2) `Restart` during `stopped` / `error` / `disconnected` skips the `await stop()` branch (since `state.connection !== "running"`) — `stop()` would itself return early if `sessionRef.current` is null, but the explicit guard makes the intent clearer + avoids a transient "Stopping…" frame in the status copy. (3) `reset()` already bumps `genRef` so any in-flight WS messages from before the click are dropped — no stale-event clobber of the new session's state. (4) `state.error` is wiped by `reset()` (which sets state to `INITIAL_DYNAMIC_TRACE_STATE`) — so `Clear overlay` also serves as the recovery path from "WebSocket dropped" / "server error" toasts. **What this is NOT.** **Not** a Frida-session-management feature — backend session lifecycle is unchanged (the existing `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic` start + `DELETE /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic/{session_id}` stop are reused verbatim). **Not** a new architectural lock — DEC-031 stays Active (closing note already appended at v3.X-next.2; v3.X-next.6 lands under DEC-031's umbrella as a small operator-recovery follow-up; the policy choice (a) strict vs (b) restart vs (c) both was implementation-detail discussion captured in the v3.X-next.6 deliverable column below, not a decision-tier lock). **Not** a new test surface — UI-affordance-only change with no logic surface; the visibility-flag computation is straightforward boolean algebra and the underlying `useDynamicTrace` state machine is already test-covered. **Not** a new dependency. **Not** a backend change. **Files touched (2 code + 3 docs).** `androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/LabTraceMode.tsx` (Restart + Clear button wiring), `androscan/web/frontend/src/App.css` (two new button-variant rules), `docs/TASKS.md` (this Active Task block + v3.X-next.6 row marked `[x]` in the Phase 13 v3.X-next backlog table), `docs/STATE.md` (Recent completed work entry + Next expected milestone flipped — see below), `docs/DECISIONS.md` (DEC-031 closing-note follow-up appended). **Next slot (operator's call):** **(1) v3.X-next.5** — shimmer for "Building trace" (FE-only, ~+25 LOC total, smallest LOC delta of any remaining sub-step); **(2) LCP.8** — chat-dock SSE provider routing (closes ISSUE-023; real operator-visible regression on llama.cpp); **(3) LCP.7** — model-sourcing UX (Settings UI), planning checkpoint pending; **(4) Phase 12** — Behavior Trace v3 static-precision menu (DEC-026 ratification gated on real-app telemetry); **(5) v3.X-next.4** — hover-expand-card (operator-demand-gated, dogfood signal); **(6) Phase 13 v3.X-next.3** — `method_invocations` gap-recovery (dogfood-gated).

Captures the regression the operator surfaced 2026-05-21 — `androscan/llm/client.py::stream_complete()` (line 295-355; used by the workbench chat SSE endpoint via `androscan/web/chat.py:631-634`) stays hard-coded to the Ollama `/api/chat` codepath, with no router on `Config.provider_kind()`, despite LCP.2 (commit `fc33658`, 2026-05-06) introducing the parallel router on `complete()`. The docstring at lines 312-314 explicitly admits the carve-out: *"Cloud providers don't currently flow through this helper; the workbench SSE endpoint is Ollama-only by design (matches the streaming/thinking semantics of the local model)."* That was accurate pre-LCP (cloud was the only alternative) but became wrong the moment llama.cpp shipped as a second LOCAL provider. Operator on `llm.provider: "llamacpp"` sees `Cannot connect to Ollama at http://localhost:11434` on every chat-dock send, even with their llama.cpp setup correct + the Settings → Status panel's LLM card reading green at 67ms with the configured GGUF loaded (because `androscan/web/health_probes.py::probe_llamacpp` only does `GET /health` + `GET /v1/models` liveness — no chat-completion round-trip). LCP.8 deliverable column captures: (a) recommended fix = sibling `_stream_llamacpp(...)` with provider-aware router on `stream_complete()` (~+150 LOC client + ~+15 tests; preserves streaming UX), (b) fallback fix = chat.py picks streamer based on `provider_kind()` (~30 LOC; loses streaming UX on llama.cpp), (c) Q1-Q5 planning-checkpoint open questions (sibling-vs-fallback / cloud-folding / probe extension / `<think>` strip semantics / test-count target). Promotion **not gated on dogfood evidence** — repro is clear + regression is real; promote LCP.8 to Active when operator schedules the LCP.8.0 planning checkpoint. **(2) New `v3.X-next.5` candidate stub row added to § Phase 13 v3.X-next — sub-step backlog** (after the v3.X-next.3 `method_invocations` gap-recovery stub): shimmer loading overlay on the "Building trace — this fires one LLM call per anchor and may take a few seconds or minutes…" copy block in `androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/LabTraceMode.tsx` (`state.kind === "building"` branch). **Phase 13 v3.X-next.2 — Behavior Trace V3 promoted to production: `method_invocations` consumer + V2 module deletion + `?flow=v3` URL gate removal + adjacent UX (N6 / N7 / N8) + FE vitest infra + 29 new FE unit tests — done 2026-05-21 (commit immediately preceding this docs-only stub-queue; implementation beat following v3.X-next.2.0's docs-only ratification immediately below; closing beat of the v3.X-next track per the Q1=(b′) split-under-path-B cadence; all five locked Q1-Q5 + adjacent N3-N8 items translated 1-1 to production).** Implementation beat on top of v3.X-next.2.0's locked Q1-Q5. **Single calendar day with v3.X-next.2.0 + v3.X-next.1 + v3.X-next.1.0 ratifications** — fourth Agent-mode session today. **Code changes (10 files touched + 1 net deletion + 1 new test file + 1 new copy module = ~+1100 FE LOC delta net, accounting for the V2 deletion):** **(1) `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/trace.ts`** (+~15 LOC) — added `CallSite` TypeScript type mirroring the BE dataclass byte-equal + `method_invocations?: Record<string, CallSite[]>` on `BehaviorAnchor`. Optional `?` per the additive-at-schema-v2 contract Q4=(b). **(2) `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/executionFlowGraphV3.ts`** (~+150 LOC net; +220 added, -70 reshaped) — new public helpers `methodKey` / `overloadKey` / `overloadKeyFromNodeId` / `closureMethodCount` moved here from the (now-deleted) V2 module so the V3 module owns the smali-signature substrate; new `ExecutionFlowV3Edge.kind: "invoke"` variant + `instructionIndex` / `inBranchOf` / `branchLabel` fields on the edge type for CFG-position-aware metadata; new `emitInvokeEdges(methodInvocations, methods, nodeIds, edges)` helper that walks the per-caller `CallSite[]` in `instruction_index` order, partitions calls per `(in_branch_of, branch_label)` arm into independent chains (each arm anchors at the caller for its first hop), suppresses self-loop edges while still advancing the chain, and returns the set of caller node ids that emitted at least one invoke edge; `buildExecutionFlowV3Graph` Phase-1 ingest now folds `method_invocations` callers + callees into the methods map (callees tagged with new `isInvokeTarget` provenance flag — see (3) below); Phase-3 edge emission calls `emitInvokeEdges` first as the primary CFG-aware channel, then falls back to the v3.1 synthesised `entry → decision-source ``call`` ` edges (Q5=(a) gap-fallback) ONLY when the entry's own invocations list is empty / missing; verdict-edge emission unchanged but rewritten to include the new edge-type fields with `null` values; `graphV3Stats` tracks `invokeEdges` in a separate bucket alongside `callEdges` + `verdictEdges` so the diagnostic overlay reads at a glance which channel is active. **(3) Provenance-split bug fix (caught by unit tests, fixed in this same commit):** initial implementation tagged `method_invocations` callees with `isPlanTarget: true` which (a) incorrectly bypassed the `gatesOnly` framework filter for invoke-only callees + (b) false-flagged every invoke target as `possiblyInlined: true` (the very opposite signal — being called via an `invoke-*` op means the slicer SAW the call, the opposite of inlined). Fixed by introducing a third provenance flag `isInvokeTarget` on `CallerCollector`; the node-survival predicate keeps `isInvokeTarget` as a keep-signal (legit non-framework invoke-only callees still render) but the framework-filter bypass stays scoped to `isEntry + isPlanTarget` only (operator-opted-in sources); `possiblyInlined` heuristic stays `!isDecisionSource && isPlanTarget` (unchanged shape but now provenance-correct). Surfaced by the `skips invoke edges whose callee was framework-filtered` unit test failing on first run; production callers don't trigger the case (slicer denylists framework callees upstream) but the FE backstop now matches its stated intent. **(4) `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/ExecutionFlowV3.tsx`** (~+330 LOC) — full dynamic-overlay surface ported from the (now-deleted) V2 component per DEC-031 N1's "scope_decision = (A) port full V2 surface in this same commit" operator pick (rejected the leaner "static-only V3 + V2 deletion + dynamic-overlay v3.X-next.4 follow-up" option). New optional props: `mode` / `firedMethods` / `liveValues` / `hookFailed` / `onSourceLineClick`. `MethodNodeV3` extended with: fired-emphasis CSS class on fired nodes when mode allows; depth pill (`d:N · t:M` + optional `× count`) rendered bottom-right on fired nodes; runtime `inlined (runtime)` pill takes precedence over the static `possibly inlined` pill when `hookFailed` lands; N7 source-line pill rendered as a clickable button when `onSourceLineClick` is wired (falls back to a plain span when omitted — graceful degradation for consumers outside `LabTraceMode`); N8 title shape swap — primary line is the method name only (`validatePin`) with the fully-qualified `MainActivity.validatePin` as the secondary line (replaces the v3.1 `MainActivity.validatePin` primary + `MainActivity` chip pattern); N6 chip relabels — `9 ?` → `9 neutral?` + `9 unv` → `9 unverdicted?` with per-sub-chip hover tooltips sourced from the new `copy.ts` constants module. `VerdictEdgeV3` extended with: fired-emphasis stroke / label-style classes; untaken-dynamic fade class when mode is dynamic; live-value chip rendered 18px below the verdict label on fired edges via the same second-`EdgeLabelRenderer` pattern V2 used. New `composeLiveLabel(LiveValueRecord)` helper ported verbatim from V2 (truncates args at 24 chars each, capped at 4 with `+N more` suffix, total chip budget 56 chars). Main `ExecutionFlowV3` function reshapes the `useMemo` block to index `firedMethods` / `liveValues` / `hookFailed` by overload key per node + computes per-edge `edgeFired = sourceFired && targetFired` (synthetic return-pill targets ride on the source's fired flag). **Phase 13 v2.0.1 hotfix — done 2026-05-19 (this commit; v2.0 commit `44e110b` was the predecessor on the same calendar day; DEC-030 closing-note appended with v2.0.1 record).** Single-file CSS hotfix on top of v2.0 addressing a clear regression operator surfaced via dogfooding within minutes of v2.0 deploying: the within-page fullscreen toggle (Q7 = (a)) only covered the top ~560 px of the viewport instead of filling the full page. **Root cause** — the base `.execution-flow-container` rule (App.css:6594-6608, in place since 13.6) pins `width: 100%` + `height: 560px` so the in-page flowchart card has a stable predictable height. The v2.0 `-fullscreen` modifier (App.css:7833-7840) added `position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 1000;` but did NOT override the base `width` / `height`. CSS spec: when `position: fixed` is set with a non-`auto` `width` or `height`, the corresponding `right` / `bottom` offsets from `inset` are ignored (the explicit dimension wins). Result: the fullscreen container fixed at `(0, 0)` with width = `100vw` but height clamped to `560px` — workbench chrome leaked through below the flowchart. **Fix** — explicit `width: 100vw; height: 100vh;` added to `.execution-flow-container-fullscreen`. Three-line addition + doc-block. Pure CSS; no JS, no schema, no test impact. **Cumulative bundle delta (v2.0.1 alone):** CSS 128.99 → 129.01 KB / **+22 bytes raw** (gzipped unchanged within round-off); JS unchanged at 1457.85 KB. **Verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean (no TS change); `vite build` clean (CSS hash bumps, JS unchanged); pytest stays at **1290 passed** (BE not touched). **Operator's explicit option (i) at the v2.0.1 planning fork:** "commit the fullscreen hotfix NOW as a tiny v2.0.1 patch on top of `44e110b`, then plan the next iteration as a separate ratification surface" — so v2.0.1 ships minimal and the three friction signals (a)+(b)+(c) go into the v2-next planning checkpoint immediately above. **`44e110b` (v2.0) → this commit (v2.0.1) → next ratification surface (v2-next).**

**Phase 13 v2.0 — Behavior Trace Flowchart UX iteration — done 2026-05-19 (this commit; DEC-030 ratified Active in this same commit).** Four operator-visible flowchart fixes landed in a single sub-step covering the four friction signals operator dogfooding 2026-05-08 → 2026-05-19 surfaced on the v1 ship: (1) multiple `NEUTRAL` sinks crowding the right edge; (2) bottom-left "fit view" button mis-read as a fullscreen affordance; (3) arrowheads invisible (v1's `fill: currentColor` on shared SVG `<marker>` didn't inherit edge stroke colour); (4) boxes appearing above the Entry method + no indentation in the column-rank layout. **Locked Q1-Q8 ratification record (mirrors DEC-030 verbatim):** Q1 = (g) hover-progressive disclosure (small de-emphasized per-source `sink_neutral` always visible at 110×36 with dashed border + 60% opacity + lowercase "neutral" body label + sink-hover tooltip listing feeding gates; new per-method-card gate-count badge `${totalGates}g · ${unclassifiedGates}?` rendered always when totalGates > 0 but CSS-hidden by default with `opacity: 0; visibility: hidden` + revealed instantly (80ms transition) on `.execution-flow-node:hover` OR `:focus-within` for keyboard discoverability; `-warn` modifier raises emphasis when at least one gate is unclassified by the heuristic `branch_classifier` — operator-spottable signal for potential false negatives per `branch_classifier.py:69-83`'s known catalog of obfuscated strings / non-English deny copy / custom-predicate gates); Q2 = (a) global ALLOW / DENY sink coalescing (new `GLOBAL_ALLOW_SINK_ID` + `GLOBAL_DENY_SINK_ID` module constants exported from `executionFlowGraph.ts`; all allow / deny verdict edges (when not routed to a `BypassPlan` target) converge on the two global sinks per anchor); Q3 = (a) `fill: context-stroke` arrowhead fix (one-line CSS change at `App.css:6856`; SVG2 keyword that correctly inherits the stroke colour of the referencing path; supported in Chrome 99+ / Firefox 90+ / Safari 16+); Q5 = (b) within-column entry-first sort (`ExecutionFlow.tsx::layoutNodes` comparator gains `kind === "entry"` short-circuit at the top of the chain; preserved v1 synthetics-last + alphabetic-between rules); Q7 = (a) within-page fullscreen toggle via React Flow `<Panel position="top-right">` + ESC keydown handler + active-class indicator + `aria-pressed` (flips a CSS class on the container expanding it to `position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 1000;` so the flowchart fills the viewport without going OS-level fullscreen — operator-locked posture: within-page only); Q4 = (c) defer call-graph injection to v2.1 (operator-demand-gated; DEC-029's locked threshold ladder lands on `DynamicTraceRunControl` (`androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/LabTraceMode.tsx`): `thresholdBand(hookCount)` pure helper bands the count into ≤ 20 = green / 21-50 = yellow / 51-100 = orange / > 100 = red; `thresholdTooltip(hookCount)` composes the operator-readable copy ("device may stutter — Frida overhead grows with the hook count" at orange / red, "within the comfortable performance band for most devices" at green / yellow). Pre-run the count comes from new `closureMethodCount(anchor)` helper exported from `executionFlowGraph.ts` (mirrors BE `extract_closure_methods`'s five-source flatten — entry method + decisions[*].method + plans[*].(target_method | source_decision_method) + advanced_plans[*].(...) — keyed on full Smali signature so overloads count separately, matching what Frida actually attempts to hook); post-run it switches to `state.readyStats.methods_attempted` (the actual BE-attempted count after `hop_cap` truncation). Button label includes the count `Run dynamic trace (N)` so the band colour is paired with the source-of-truth number; running-state class neutralises the band tint via cascade so the operator-targeting "should I run this" colour switches off once the trace is up. Runtime-confirmed inlined wiring threads the existing 13.8 `state.hookFailed: ReadonlyMap<overloadKey, HookFailureRecord>` into `<ExecutionFlow>` and `<Inspector>`: `ExecutionFlow.tsx::MethodNode` now reads `data.runtimeInlined` (built from the same overload-key index pattern 13.8 uses for `firedMethods` / `liveValues`) and renders the warn-orange dashed-border + saturated-tint card emphasis (`.execution-flow-node-inlined-runtime`) plus a louder `inlined (runtime)` pill (`.execution-flow-node-inlined-pill-runtime`) that takes precedence over the cool-gray `possibly inlined` heuristic pill via a render-side ternary; `Inspector.tsx`'s Signature section runs the same precedence — runtime-confirmed pill replaces the static heuristic pill (never both, avoids double-decoration), the callout body upgrades from muted-small heuristic copy to warn-orange `.inspector-inlined-callout-runtime` with the operator-actionable "R8 most likely inlined the method into its callers; try hooking the caller method instead" message + the Frida `reason` + optional `error` rendered as a code chip + muted span. Chat-widget renderer for `summarise_method` ships as the second consumer of the `SkillResult.widgets` channel after Phase 11 v2.1.5's `<TraceEntryCandidateWidget>`: new `MethodSummaryWidget` dataclass added to `androscan/skills/base.py` (frozen, `kind: Literal["method_summary"]`, six fields: `class_smali` / `class_name` / `method_name` / `descriptor` / `summary` / `cached`); `SkillWidget = Union[TraceEntryCandidateWidget, MethodSummaryWidget]` extension is additive-by-design so the FE's `<ChatWidgetRenderer>` graceful-fallback survives any future schema additions. `summarise_method.py::execute()` now emits a `MethodSummaryWidget` on BOTH the cache-hit path (`cached=True`) AND the fresh-LLM path (`cached=False`); failed paths (missing required args, LLM exception, empty LLM response) emit no widget so the chat dock doesn't render an interactive card on a failed summary. New helpers `smali_class_to_java_dotted(class_smali)` (strips inner-class suffix, returns `""` on malformed input — fail-open posture) + `build_method_summary_widget(class_smali, method_name, descriptor, summary_text, cached)` exported from the skill so tests can pin the widget shape directly. New FE type `MethodSummaryWidgetData` in `types.ts` mirrors the BE dataclass field-for-field; `ChatWidget = TraceEntryCandidateWidgetData | MethodSummaryWidgetData`. New component `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/chat/widgets/MethodSummaryWidget.tsx` (~165 LOC) renders the card: Smali signature header + `(cached)` muted pill when applicable + summary paragraph + three action buttons matching the Inspector's action row (Hook this method / Trace this gate / Open source) — all three use the existing cross-surface pendings (`pendingHookPrefill` with `entry_exit_log` template / `pendingTraceEntry` with the full Smali signature / `pendingCodeNav` to Inspect tab) so the chat-card and the Inspector show the same actions and land the operator on the same Lab / Inspect surface. `<ChatWidgetRenderer>` switch arm registered for `case "method_summary"`. Inspector "Discuss in chat" affordance: new button below the Summary section's state-driven body (`onDiscussInChat`) writes `pendingChatPrefill` with a method-specific prompt template that pre-arms the agentic loop with the four `summarise_method` params (class_smali / method_name / descriptor / app_id) embedded as Markdown-quoted strings in the message body — operator reviews / sends, the LLM picks `summarise_method`, the skill returns text + the new `MethodSummaryWidget` alongside, and the chat dock renders the interactive card on the same assistant turn. Always visible (not gated on summary state) so the operator can ask about a method even before its summary has landed or after one has failed. **Backend.** Three small surfaces: `MethodSummaryWidget` dataclass + `SkillWidget` union extension + the skill's two `execute()`-branch widget emissions + two new exported helpers (`smali_class_to_java_dotted` + `build_method_summary_widget`); zero changes to the WS-handler-tier (`_build_summarise_method_callable` only reads `result.text`, ignores `widgets`). **Tests.** `tests/test_summarise_method_skill.py` +8 tests in new `TestPhase13_9_MethodSummaryWidget` class (3 cases for `smali_class_to_java_dotted` — top-level / inner-class / malformed; (2) static analysis alone can't tell the operator what *actually* fired at runtime — operators ask "is this start-to-finish?" / "which methods actually run when I tap Login?", questions only a runtime trace can answer. **Locked design (per DEC-029, ratified 2026-05-08 after a three-round plan-mode iteration over the WeakBank `MainActivity.login(…)` example):** three coherent deliverables — (1) UI overhaul (rename "Decision Timeline" → "Behavior Trace"; replace linear timeline with a flowchart + Inspector pane; remove predicate-register / instruction-index / neutral-badge noise from the primary UI); (2) Dynamic execution trace via a new `behavior_trace_multi` Frida template + tiered arg/return serialiser + WebSocket multiplexing for live trace events + summary events on the existing `/ws/trace` channel; (3) Per-method LLM summaries via a new `summarise_method` skill, live-on-first-hit, cached in `skill_results_cache`. **Edge styling locked at color-only emphasis** (same 1.5px stroke, same 6×6 arrowhead for all edges; fired = accent blue + full opacity; untaken = dim/dashed at 55% opacity) — the plan-mode iteration explicitly tested thicker fired edges and a larger arrowhead and the operator rejected both ("Blue arrow looks fine, but don't make it thick"). **Three modes** (`Static` default before any dynamic run, `Dynamic`, `Both` overlay default after first dynamic run). **Run-trace button color thresholds** (≤20 hooks green / 21-50 yellow / 51-100 orange / >100 red). **Canonical mockup** at `~/.cursor/projects/Users-sujoy-chakravarti-TW-Work-AI-security-Cursor-AI-AndroScan/canvases/phase-13-trace-mockup.canvas.tsx`. **Sub-step backlog 13.0 → 13.10** (strictly linear, one sub-step per Agent-mode session; mirrors Phase 11's 11.0 → 11.7 cadence). 13.0 (this commit) ships docs + canvas only (DEC-029 ratified, Phase 13 sub-step backlog table added, `STATE.md` stub, canvas mockup written). **13.1 next** — preceded by a brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint to lock the multi-method tracer JS template shape (per-method `Java.use()` failure handling, depth-counter implementation, arg/return serialiser tier ordering) before the template ships. **Phase 12 / DEC-026 stays untouched** — operator direction was explicit ("Ignore phase 12 stuff for now, we'll come to it later after phase 13"); **Historical LCP.6 spec (as ratified at LCP.0; preserved for the as-built record):** Strict-committed follow-up per DEC-027 Q2 (a) lock-in, NOT operator-demand-gated. Emit GBNF grammar from the existing JSON schema for `skill_requests` (discriminated union over registered skill names, each with their own `params` shape) + `hypotheses` (with optional `widgets[]` post-v2.1.5). Emitter lives in new `androscan/llm/grammar.py` (~+200 LOC including per-skill schema introspection + tests). Forwarded as a per-request `grammar` field to llama.cpp's `/v1/chat/completions` (llama.cpp-specific extension to the OpenAI-compat shim) and as a `format: <json-schema>` payload to Ollama (newer builds supporting JSON-schema mode; fall back to `format: "json"` on older builds — version detection in `health_probes.probe_ollama`). **Single LCP.6 vs LCP.6a + LCP.6b split decision** lives in the LCP.6 planning checkpoint sub-bullet of `docs/STATE.md` based on emitter scope at that point. **As-built outcome:** Single commit, no split (per-skill `params_schema` is operator-prose not JSON Schema, so emitter scope shrank ~5x). Both Ollama JSON-schema mode (since v0.5.0) and llama.cpp GBNF wired with opportunistic 400-fallback. ISSUE-016 closed.

**Historical context (kept for the as-built record — was the active framing at LCP.0 ratification):** LCP track ratified at LCP.0 (commit `52e4f8e`, 2026-05-06) as a parallel infrastructure track on `main`, not a Phase — closest precedent is CI.0. **DEC-027** locked the seven-sub-step backlog LCP.0 → LCP.6 (strictly linear, one sub-step per Agent-mode session, mirrors the Phase 10 / 11 / v2.1 cadence operators are familiar with). **Scope:** swap the local LLM runtime from Ollama (`qwen3.5:35b` heavy on a 36 GB M3 unified-memory budget per the operator's 2026-05-05 status share) to **llama.cpp** (specifically `llama-server`'s OpenAI-compatible HTTP shim) — operator runs `llama-server -c 16384 -ngl 99 -fa --port 8033 --host 127.0.0.1 --jinja` against an UD-Q5_K_XL / Q5_K_M Qwen3-family GGUF; AndroScan consumes `http://127.0.0.1:8033/v1/chat/completions` via a new dedicated `_call_llamacpp()` HTTP path parallel to `_call_ollama()`. **Locked decisions per DEC-027 (Q1-Q5 ratification record):** Q1 (B + table refactor) — dedicated `_call_llamacpp()` codepath using `requests` directly (NOT extending `_call_cloud()`'s `openai` SDK path) + restructure `CLOUD_PROVIDERS` → top-level `LLM_PROVIDERS` dict with `LLM_PROVIDERS["local"]` (containing `"ollama"` + `"llamacpp"`) and `LLM_PROVIDERS["cloud"]` (containing the existing 6 cloud providers verbatim) sub-sections + per-entry `kind` field (`"local-ollama"` | `"local-openai-compat"` | `"cloud"`) + new `Config.provider_kind() -> Literal[...]` method (`is_cloud()` preserved as a backwards-compat alias) + backwards-compat `CLOUD_PROVIDERS = LLM_PROVIDERS["cloud"]` alias to keep mid-refactor commits clean; Q2 (a → defer GBNF to LCP.6, but **promote LCP.6 from optional / telemetry-gated → committed follow-up**) — v1 ships `response_format: {"type": "json_object"}` (Ollama parity); GBNF grammar enforcement for `skill_requests` / `hypotheses` schema lands as committed LCP.6 follow-up benefitting **all** local providers (Ollama supports JSON-schema mode in newer builds); Q3 (B) — Settings UI top-level radio "Local (Ollama)" / "Local (llama.cpp)" / "Cloud" with nested cloud dropdown (replaces the existing flat provider dropdown); LCP.4 may split into 4a (UI restructure, Ollama defaults preserved) + 4b (llama.cpp sub-knobs land) if the diff balloons; Q4 (A) — default `base_url: http://127.0.0.1:8033/v1` (loopback-explicit per DEC-006 / DEC-021 lineage; port `8033` is operator-chosen non-default — operator passes `--port 8033` to `llama-server`); Q5 (C) — README pointer only at LCP.5 (no `docs/LLAMACPP.md` operator setup guide; smallest surface, symmetric posture with the existing Ollama dependency that has no in-repo guide either). **Branch posture:** ship on `main` with selective-staging discipline (rejected the alternative of a feature branch — operator chose the established Phase 10 / 11 / v2.1 sub-step-by-sub-step cadence after the v2.1 follow-up release fully landed and `main` became quiescent at HEAD `15e7c1a`). **Sub-step backlog:** LCP.0 (this commit, planning checkpoint + DEC-027, docs only); LCP.1 (`LLM_PROVIDERS` table refactor + `Config.provider_kind()` + `CLOUD_PROVIDERS` backwards-compat alias — pure refactor); LCP.2 (`_call_llamacpp()` + router branch in `complete()` + JSON-mode parity + `<think>` strip — backend behavior addition); LCP.3 (`probe_llamacpp` + Status panel block); LCP.4 (Settings UI radio restructure + llama.cpp sub-knobs); LCP.5 (docs sweep + DEC-027 closing note + KNOWN_ISSUES GBNF deferral entry + README pointer); LCP.6 (committed GBNF grammar enforcement follow-up — benefits all local providers). **Risks:** JSON-validity drift on aggressive quants (Q4_K_M / IQ4_XS) addressed by LCP.6's GBNF; LCP.4 is the only sub-step with non-trivial main-branch rollback cost (operator-visible Settings UX restructure) — 4a/4b split provides cheap rollback story. See § **LCP — llama.cpp local provider — sub-step backlog** below for the strictly-linear sub-step table.

## Interactive RE Workbench (Phases 6–9)

Planned work: web-based interactive reverse engineering on top of the existing CLI pipeline — emulator mirror, click-to-code, static call graph from Smali, Frida tracing/hooking. **Stack (target):** FastAPI + WebSocket backend, React + Vite + TypeScript frontend, Monaco Editor, Cytoscape.js.

> **Naming note (post-DEC-024, 2026-04-28; code rename completed 2026-04-29):** the tab labelled **Hook Lab** in Phase 6→9 prose below is renamed **Lab** going forward, and the code-level rename (`HookLabTab.tsx` → `LabTab.tsx`, `HookLabCodeView` → `LabCodeView`, `#/hook` URL hash → `#/lab` with one-shot back-compat redirect, `tab="hook"` chat id → `tab="lab"` with `_normalise_tab` back-compat alias) shipped in sub-step **10.6**. Historical references to "Hook Lab" (this section's UX step 4 list, the `### Hook Lab v1 — sub-step backlog` table, completed-task entries) are accurate proper nouns for the v1 milestone and stay as-is. New work uses **Lab**. See **DEC-024** for the full policy.

**Phase 6→9 UX rollout (in addition to backend phases above):**

1. **[done]** Tab shell + `react-resizable-panels` + `WorkbenchContext` + `ChatDock` skeleton.
2. **[done]** Reports tab refresh + chat back-end. `androscan/web/triage.py` (`apps/<app>/<run>/triage.json`); `androscan/web/chat.py` with layered guardrails (allowlisted system prompt per tab, length caps, ANSI/zero-width/secret redaction, per-kind attachment budgets, `<context>` prompt-injection wrapping, rate limit, transcript JSONL). LLM client extended (`response_format=None`, `messages` override). Frontend `FindingCard` with Markdown + per-card triage buttons + severity override + note; `ChatDock` wired to `/api/chat`. **Note:** Lane-1 RAG indexer for static decompiled code is **deferred to step 3** where it pairs with click-to-code mapping (the chat needs code retrieval there; Reports works on dossier + finding alone).
3. Inspect tab — **done:**
   - **[done]** Lane-1 RAG indexer over decompiled sources (one-shot after jadx finishes). New `androscan/rag/` package: brace-balanced chunker for `.java`/`.kt`, `EmbedProvider` protocol with `fastembed` (default ONNX) / `ollama` / `hash` (test fallback) implementations, per-app SQLite vector store (WAL, packed `float32` blobs) at `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/rag.sqlite`, brute-force cosine top-k (numpy when present, pure-Python fallback). Endpoints `GET /api/rag/{app_id}/status`, `POST /api/rag/{app_id}/rebuild`, `POST /api/rag/{app_id}/query`. Auto-built via `schedule_rag_build_after_decompile` when the decompile cache turns ready. New `llm`-tier skill `search_decompiled_sources` (query, top_k, package_prefix, file_substr) advertised in the prompt catalog and fail-open. Inspect-tab chat enrichment in `androscan/web/chat.py::_enrich_inspect_with_rag` appends top-k snippets as `code` attachments (fail-soft when RAG is unavailable). New `pyproject` extra `[rag]` (fastembed, numpy). `Config` adds `rag_embed_provider` / `rag_embed_model` / `rag_top_k_default` (YAML `rag.*`, env `ANDROSCAN_RAG_*`). 197 tests pass.
   - **[done]** Package-scoped logcat backend (`/ws/logcat?package=…`) using **stable Linux-UID filtering** (`adb logcat --uid=<uid>` resolved via `stat /data/data/<pkg>` or `dumpsys package`) so the stream survives app restarts; falls back to `--pid=` re-resolution on older devices.
   - **[done]** Mirror online/offline probe at `GET /api/device/status` (`adb get-state`); UI badge wiring still pending.
   - **[done]** Click-to-map backend (`POST /api/inspect/map` in `androscan/web/inspect_map.py`): `dumpsys activity top` for foreground activity, `uiautomator dump /dev/tty` for view hierarchy, smallest-area-clickable bounds match, regex grep over the persistent decompile cache for handler candidates (`findViewById` > `onClick_near` > `compose_id` > `reference`). Persistent decompile cache backend in `androscan/web/decompile_cache.py` (`POST /api/decompile/{app_id}`, `GET /api/code/{app_id}/{tree,file}`) keyed by `sha256(apk)` under `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/sources/`. Sandboxed `POST /api/adb/shell` proxy with `shlex` parsing + denylist + 20 s timeout + 200 KB output cap.
   - **[done]** New `llm`-tier skill `resolve_ui_element` (`androscan/skills/resolve_ui_element.py`) — deterministic, explainable scorer over raw click-to-code candidates with optional Lane-1 RAG enrichment (synthesises a query from `text` > `content_desc` > short resource id). Pure-function `resolve()` reused inline by `/api/inspect/map` so the response now carries a `resolution` block ({`best`, `alternatives`, `rag_hits`, `reasoning`}) without an LLM round-trip. Tests: `tests/test_resolve_ui_element.py` (13 tests). **210 tests total, all passing.**
   - **[done]** Inspect-tab **frontend** wiring (`androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/InspectTab.tsx` + components). Four-column resizable layout: Projects sidebar | Classes-&-methods tree + scoped logcat | UI-mapping/Code-browser tabs + chat dock | Mirror + adb-shell. `MirrorView` renders `/ws/mirror` PNG frames, polls `/api/device/status` for an online/offline status dot, translates clicks from image-pixel space to device-pixel space and forwards to `/api/inspect/map`. `ElementMappingPanel` shows a sub-activity progress bar during the map call and then renders the fused `resolution.best` as a prominent banner (`SourceBadge` regex/RAG pill, kind pill, score, reasons grid, snippet, "Open in Code browser"), the deterministic candidates list with kind pills + per-row "Open" buttons, plus collapsible `ResolutionAlternatives` and `RagHitsList` (with the synthesised RAG query). The Code-browser tab uses `CodeView` (line numbers, scroll-to-line, persistent line-range highlight, find query, gear prefs); a tap auto-opens `resolution.best.file` and scrolls/highlights the picked line range, falling back to `candidates[0]` when no fuser pick exists. Chat attachments (`attachments` memo in `InspectTab`) include the fused `best_handler` JSON block alongside the element + candidates so the LLM sees the picked file/line/reasons. New CSS for `.source-badge` (deterministic = green, rag = warn), `.best-banner` (accent border + soft background, reasons grid), `.resolution-alts`, `.rag-hits`. Vite production build clean (308 modules, 32 KB CSS, 393 KB JS).
3.5. **[done]** Settings tab — fourth top-level tab (`#/settings`) with four sub-panels:
   - **Global settings:** form view (with source pills `yaml`/`env`/`default` per field, env-lock indicator, restart-required pill on non-live-reloadable fields) **plus** a raw-YAML editor with server-side validation; "Reset to defaults" button.
   - **App settings (per-app):** project dropdown; per-key override-or-inherit toggles writing to `apps/<app_id>/app_settings.json` (atomic); "Reset to defaults" with force-with-warning when an override would diverge from a global field still consumed via the boot-time closure.
   - **Status:** live cards for global health (adb / jadx / apktool / frida / Ollama / embed-provider / disk free + writability) and per-app health (`pm path`, foreground activity, UID, uiautomator dump, apk-sha drift, RAG status, decompile cache freshness); "Refresh now" cache-bypass button.
   - **Diagnostics:** raw API JSON for global + per-app payloads, `POST /api/settings/reload`.
   - Backend modules: `androscan/web/health_probes.py` (timeboxed pure-function async probes — never raise, return `{ok, label, ...}`), `androscan/web/per_app_settings.py` (atomic schema-versioned writes, `effective_settings` merger), `androscan/web/status_routes.py` (`asyncio.gather` + 3 s in-process cache; `invalidate_status_cache()` on every settings save), `androscan/web/settings_routes.py` (validates raw YAML, `dump_to_yaml`, `restore_defaults_yaml`; returns `restart_required` per field). `androscan/config/loader.py` extended with `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP`, `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS`, `global_view_from_config`, `effective_sources`, `with_overrides`, `coerce_yaml_value`, `dump_to_yaml`, `save_raw_yaml`, `restore_defaults_yaml`, `validate_raw_yaml`, `read_raw_yaml`, `discover_config_path`. `app.py` keeps the live `Config` on `app.state.config` (`_current_config`/`_set_config` helpers) so live-reloadable fields take effect without restarting uvicorn.
   - Frontend: `SettingsTab` + sub-panels, `HealthDot` in the global header (30 s polling, deep-link to Settings), `api/settings.ts`, `api/status.ts`, ~300 lines of new CSS in `App.css`.
   - Tests: `tests/test_health_probes.py`, `tests/test_per_app_settings.py`, `tests/test_settings_routes.py`. All Python tests green; `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean.
   - See **DEC-020** (settings tab design) and **DEC-021** (probe shape + asyncio.gather + cache).
4. Hook Lab tab: static smali call graph, Frida adapter + ring buffer + agentic tool calls (`frida_query`, `frida_stats`), Cytoscape overlay (static = muted grey, frida = bold cyan), Monaco decompiled + script editor, scope/hooks inspector, chat with frida summary + tail. **Implementation plan locked in DEC-023** (planning commit 2026-04-25); see § **Hook Lab v1 — sub-step backlog** below for the strictly-linear 8-sub-step rollout.

The JS hooks every overload via `Java.use`, walks `this.getClass().getDeclaredFields()` per call (with `Field.setAccessible(true)` and per-field try/catch so a single getter that throws never voids the whole snapshot), and emits two new `send` payload variants the aggregator pattern-matches on: `{phase:"entry", class, method, args, this_class, this_fields:{f: String(v), ...}}` + `{phase:"exit", class, method, return, this_fields:{...}}`. Read-only by design — DEC-023's modify-return UI is still v2. Two new pure helpers in `androscan/web/frida_routes.py`: `_summarize_hooks(session)` (iterates `session.events()` once, groups by `(payload.class, payload.method)`, counts entry events as hits, tallies exit `return` strings into `top_returns: [{value, count}]` capped at 5 with stable insertion-order tiebreak, sorts rows by hit count desc → last-seen desc; returns capped per-value at 256 chars so a 200 KB stringified buffer can't blow up the response) + `_summarize_scope(session)` (additionally filters on `payload.this_fields` being a dict so other templates' entry/exit events are explicitly *excluded* from the Scope panel; keeps the most-recent entry + most-recent exit per `(class, method)` independently so a method that's mid-call still shows useful entry data). Two new GET routes wired through `build_frida_router(...)`: `GET /api/frida/sessions/{session_id}/hooks` returns `{session_id, hooks: [...]}`, `GET /api/frida/sessions/{session_id}/scope` returns `{session_id, snapshots: [...]}`. Both 404 on unknown session, both work *without* the `[frida]` extra installed (they only read the ring buffer — no Frida I/O). Frontend: new `HookStatsPanel.tsx` (polls `/hooks` every 2.5 s; renders one row per `(class, method)` with hits / last-seen / template badge / top-3 return values; gracefully handles 404-on-detach as empty state) + new `ScopeInspectorPanel.tsx` (polls `/scope` every 2.5 s; renders one collapsible `<details>` card per `(class, method)` with side-by-side entry/exit panels; computes the entry-vs-exit `this_fields` diff client-side and highlights changed keys + surfaces a "N mutated" pill on the card header). `HookLabTab.tsx`'s right-pane bottom slot is now a 3-button tab strip — `Trace | Hooks | Scope` — defaulting to Trace so existing operators see no behaviour change. Hooks + Scope tabs are disabled when no session is active (with a tooltip pointing the operator at Inject); all three panels share the same `activeSession` state so flipping tabs preserves selection. ~360 lines of new CSS in `App.css` namespaced under `.right-pane-tabs-*` / `.hookstats-*` / `.scope-*` (keeps the grep-affordance from 4.5 intact). **Polling vs. WS rationale:** `/hooks` and `/scope` are pure aggregations over the *same* ring buffer the trace WS consumes; a parallel WS would be redundant and the 2.5 s lag is invisible to a human eye scanning a summary table. Polling also means a paused trace WS doesn't also pause the Hooks/Scope panels — the underlying ring keeps receiving events. **+9 backend tests** in `tests/test_frida_routes.py` (empty session, multi-hook aggregation with top_returns ordering, malformed-payload defensiveness, scope filters out non-this_fields events, latest-entry-and-exit kept per method, plus 404 cases) + **+1 in `tests/test_frida_hook_templates.py`** (`scope_inspector` smoke render asserting JS contains `getDeclaredFields` + `setAccessible` + `this_fields` + the three phases + summary mentions `read-only` / `field` / `this_fields`); the registry-walk fail-closed test from 4.4 auto-parametrises over the new template adding **+7 structural-invariant tests** for free (id matches basename, JS + summary non-empty, both `str.format` placeholder sets ⊆ declared params, every required param appears in JS *or* summary, template renders cleanly with stub inputs). **Brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint at the top of 4.1** to settle the SQLite schema (tables, indices, foreign keys) before code lands — otherwise the Agent will improvise a schema and 4.2's query API may force a rework.

**Risks (per DEC-023):**
- JS pre-validation = **`pyjsparser`** (pure-Python, no Node.js dep). Inject button stays disabled until the rendered JS parses; parser errors shown inline.
- Device-touching tests are gated by `pytest -m device` (opt-in marker; not part of the default suite). `pytest -m "not device"` is the CI default.

**Settings tab follow-ups (incremental, P2):**
- Migrate hot-path route handlers (`/api/llm/info`, chat, RAG, decompile) to read from `app.state.config` so live reload covers their fields too — currently they capture `config` at boot, which is why the UI shows a `restart_required` pill for those fields.
- Add a "compare to global" diff view in the per-app settings panel so overrides are visible at a glance.
- Persist the raw-YAML editor's draft locally (sessionStorage) so an accidental tab switch doesn't lose unsaved work.
- **[done 2026-04-27, in Hook Lab 4.3]** Add a "Hook Lab readiness" rollup probe (frida-server present on device, frida CLI on host, target package gadget injectable) once Hook Lab work begins. Two-card design landed in Hook Lab 4.3: existing `tools.frida` (host CLI; from `probe_frida_version`) + new `tools.frida_server` (device-side reachability + host/server version-skew, severity `None` / `"minor"` / `"major"`); see `androscan/web/health_probes.py::probe_frida_server` + `probe_frida_version_skew` and `status_routes.py`. Treated as **yellow** in `rollupGlobal` (non-critical for static-only workflows). "Target package gadget injectable" check is deferred to 4.5 along with the Inject UI it would gate.

**RE Workbench chat — agentic skill loop (P2, planned per DEC-022):**
- Extend `androscan/web/chat.py` with a bounded `while turn < MAX_CHAT_TURNS` loop that calls `parse_response()` + `run_skills()` whenever the LLM emits `skill_requests` (mirroring `androscan/internal/workflow.py`'s pattern). Hard caps in code: `MAX_CHAT_TURNS = 5`, `MAX_SKILLS_PER_TURN = 3`, per-skill timeout = 5 s, per-turn skill-output budget ≈ 6 KB.
- Add `requires_confirmation: bool = False` to `SkillMeta`. Today's read-only LLM-tier skills stay `False`; Hook-Lab-introduced skills (frida hook injection, anything that mutates device or files) ship `True`.
- Extend the SSE vocabulary with `skill_request` / `skill_result` / `skill_pending` event types; render as collapsible cards inside the existing thinking block in `ChatDock`. For `skill_pending` (consent-required skills), the loop awaits `POST /api/chat/skill_decision/{request_id}` (Allow / Deny + optional edited args) with a 90 s TTL on pending state.
- Per-tab "always confirm" toggle in Settings → Per-app overrides (default off); when on, every skill is treated as `requires_confirmation=True` for that tab.
- Transcript schema: interleave `{type: "skill_call", turn, name, args, result_preview, duration_ms, decision?}` records into `apps/<app>/<run>/chat/<tab>.jsonl`.
- Ship behind a per-tab feature flag (`chat.agentic_loop.enabled`) so it can ship dark and enable per tab as confidence grows. Order: Inspect → Reports → Hook Lab. New tests in `tests/test_chat_agentic.py` covering happy path, max-turn cutoff, skill timeout, skill error mid-loop, consent deny, consent TTL expiry, SSE event ordering.
- Quick win to land independently: bump `_INSPECT_RAG_TOP_K` from 4 → 8–10 + raise `_INSPECT_RAG_PER_HIT_CHARS` proportionally. Reduces the failure rate that motivates this DEC but does not replace it.

**§ Phase 10 — Behavior Trace v1 — sub-step backlog** *(strictly linear; one sub-step per Agent-mode session; locked in **DEC-024** — concurrent with the Hook Lab → Lab rename)*

Phase 10 ships **Behavior Trace** as the new headline mode of the **Lab** tab (renamed from Hook Lab per DEC-024). The workflow is *Anchor → Locate → Trace → Classify → Manipulate → Verify* — operator picks a UI element or method anchor, the static layer enumerates the closure of conditional gates that govern its behaviour (≤ 3 hops by default), the LLM interprets and proposes template-bound bypass plans, the operator stages → injects via the existing Hook Lab v1 Stage→Inject path. Trace adds **zero new device-touching surface area** — all dynamic effects flow through `generate_frida_hook` + the Stage→Inject UI shipped in DEC-023 sub-steps 4.5 / 4.7. See **DEC-024** for the platform-neutral data model, the static→LLM split, the closure / hop / methods caps, the bypass-planner template binding, the risk taxonomy, and the rename policy.

| 10.1 | **[x] Decision-point extraction** *(landed 2026-04-28)* | New module `androscan/analysis/decisions.py` — third Smali pass over the same apktool tree `smali_parser.py` already walks twice (no edits to existing modules). `parse_decisions(roots, classes) -> (list[MethodDecisions], DecisionsParseSummary)` emits one `MethodDecisions` per method with at least one branch (linear methods excluded; full enumeration via the existing `ClassDecl.methods` list). Recognises all 12 if-* opcodes (`if-eq` / `if-ne` / `if-lt` / `if-le` / `if-gt` / `if-ge` / `if-eqz` / `if-nez` / `if-ltz` / `if-lez` / `if-gtz` / `if-gez`) plus `packed-switch` and `sparse-switch`. Switch data blocks parsed inline via deferred-back-fill (`_pending_switches` in `_MethodState`); each switch emits one `Branch` per case label + a `("default", None)` fall-through. Per-method `label_index: tuple[(label_name, instruction_index), ...]` lets 10.3's basic-block walker resolve any branch target without re-parsing. Instruction indexing is conservative — only lowercase-opcode lines advance the index, so labels point at real next-instruction positions. Fail-soft on malformed switch blocks via `_DecisionParseError` → `DecisionsParseSummary.parse_errors`; rest of file keeps parsing. Platform-neutral data model in new `androscan/analysis/trace_types.py`: `MethodRef` + `FieldRef` (Java-form names + raw Smali descriptors with `smali_signature` round-trip joining `call_graph.nodes`); `DecisionKind` (string-valued enum; `is_two_register_predicate` / `is_switch` accessors); `Branch(label, target_label: Optional[str])` (`None` = fall-through); `DecisionPoint(method, instruction_index, source_line, kind, predicate_registers, branches)`; `BehaviorAnchor` + `BypassPlan` shipped as minimal shells locking field names 10.4 / 10.5 will populate without breaking the contract. **13 tests** in `tests/test_decisions_extract.py` covering MethodRef + FieldRef round-trip + ValueError, linear-method exclusion, summary counters, all 12 if-* kinds (zero + two-register variants in dedicated coverage methods), source-line attachment, label_index resolution, packed-switch + sparse-switch branch emission, sparse-switch key-string preservation (`"case 0xa"`), multi-dex coverage via `smali_classes2/HiddenGate.smali`, and `DecisionPoint` JSON round-trip via `dataclasses.asdict` + `json.dumps`. Three new fixture .smali files under `tests/fixtures/trace_smali/`. Full suite **614 / 614 green** (was 601; +13). No FE changes, no schema changes, no new optional deps. | DEC-024 |
| 10.2 | **[x] Backward slicing for predicate origin** *(landed 2026-04-28)* | New module `androscan/analysis/slicing.py` — pure intra-procedural backward slicer. `slice_predicate_origins(method_decisions, *, max_walk=64) -> MethodDecisions` (frozen-dataclass-replace, no input mutation) plus `slice_one_decision(decision, instructions)` for 10.5's per-anchor walk. Five `*Origin` dataclasses added to `androscan/analysis/trace_types.py`: `MethodCallOrigin(method, invoke_kind)`, `FieldReadOrigin(field, is_static)`, `ConstOrigin(value, smali_op)`, `ParamOrigin(register)`, `CompositeOrigin(reason)`. Each carries a `kind` discriminator field for clean JSON round-trip. `DecisionPoint` extended with `predicate_origin: Optional[PredicateOrigin] = None` (additive default → 10.1 tests still pass). Walker follows `move(-wide\|-object)?(/from16\|/16)?` chains transparently; `move-result-*` looks back one step for the preceding `invoke-*` and emits MethodCallOrigin from its target signature; `iget-*` / `sget-*` emit FieldReadOrigin; `const(-*)?` emits ConstOrigin with literal text preserved verbatim; `move-exception` and any unrecognised defining opcode emit CompositeOrigin with the opcode as `reason`. Skip-set excludes `iput-*` / `sput-*` / `aput-*` / `invoke-*` / `goto*` / `return*` / `throw` / `monitor-*` / `check-cast` / `nop` / `fill-array-data` / `filled-new-array*`. Walk exhaustion via `max_walk` → None honestly per DEC-024. Walk reaches start-of-method with a `pN` register tracked → ParamOrigin; with a `vN` tracked → None (defensive). **Two-register policy:** slicer slices each operand independently then combines via `MethodCall > FieldRead > Param > Const > Composite > None`; ties to LHS for stable source-order results. **Small amendment to `decisions.py`:** `MethodDecisions` gained optional `instructions: tuple[str, ...] = ()` populated via a tiny `_emit_instruction(raw)` helper in `_MethodState` (single source of truth for the index counter + instructions list). **16 tests** in `tests/test_decisions_slicing.py`: `predicate_origin` defaults to None on 10.1 output; `instructions` populated and pointing at if-* / switch lines at the right indices; per-variant slicer assertions for all 5 origin kinds (with `iget` + `sget` separately covered for FieldRead); arithmetic + instance-of + move-exception for Composite; move-chain follows aliases; two-register const-vs-field returns FieldRead; two-register both-MethodCall returns LHS; max_walk exhaustion returns None (and same method with full walk resolves); JSON round-trip per variant verifies the `kind` discriminator. New fixture `tests/fixtures/trace_smali/smali/com/trace/Slices.smali` (~13 methods + helpers). Full suite **630 / 630 green** (was 614; +16). No FE / schema / new-deps changes. | DEC-024 |
| 10.3 | **[x] Heuristic branch outcome classifier** *(landed 2026-04-29)* | New module `androscan/analysis/branch_classifier.py` — pure deterministic classifier. Public API: `classify_branch_outcomes(method_decisions) -> MethodDecisions` (frozen-dataclass-replace, mirrors 10.2's slicer API exactly so 10.5 can chain `parse_decisions → slice_predicate_origins → classify_branch_outcomes` without bookkeeping); `classify_one_decision(decision, instructions, label_index)` for tests / 10.5's per-anchor walk. Two new dataclasses in `androscan/analysis/trace_types.py`: `BranchVerdict(branch_label, verdict, score, reasons)` and `BranchOutcome(verdicts, confidence, reasons)`. `DecisionPoint` extended with `branch_outcome: Optional[BranchOutcome] = None` (additive default; 10.1 + 10.2 tests still green). **Heuristic catalog (locked v1):** strong DENY (-1.0): `throw vN` / `Ljava/lang/System;->exit` / `Landroid/os/Process;->killProcess`. Strong ALLOW (+1.0): `L...;->setResult(...)`. Moderate DENY (-0.7): `L...;->finish\|finishAffinity\|finishAndRemoveTask()V` *only when no preceding `setResult` in the same basic block* (legitimate "report-then-close" allow-flow suppressed via order-aware walk); `const-string vN, "..."` matching the curated deny-keyword regex. Moderate ALLOW (+0.7): `L...;->startActivity(ForResult)?(...)`. Weak DENY (-0.3): branch-length ratio ≥ 3:1 with shorter ≤ 3 instructions, only when both branches are otherwise neutral, only for 2-branch ifs (early-exit-gate pattern). **Verdict thresholds:** `score ≤ -0.5 → "deny"`, `score ≥ +0.5 → "allow"`, else `"neutral"`. **Confidence tiers** based on `max(|branch.score|)`: `≥ 1.0 → 1.00` / `≥ 0.7 → 0.85` / `≥ 0.3 → 0.45` (below `LLM_RECLASSIFY_THRESHOLD = 0.6` → 10.5 LLM re-classifies) / else `0.00`. Pre-classify `branch_outcome=None`; post-classify always populated even for all-neutral / 0.0-confidence cases so 10.5 distinguishes "not run" from "run, found nothing". **Curated deny-keyword regex** extends DEC-024's spec with two strict-additive improvements: `\b...\b` boundaries (no `rootView` false-positive on `root`); optional `(?:ed\|ing)?` / `(?:ger\|ging)?` suffixes on `tamper` / `root` / `debug` (the canonical "Device is rooted" / "App was tampered" detection strings now match — without these the spec would miss them). **Basic-block walker** stops at first terminator (`return*` / `throw` / `goto*` / `if-*` / `*-switch`); terminator IS part of the block (so `throw` itself is scoreable in-place). Fall-through branches start at `decision_instruction_index + 1`. **+19 tests** in `tests/test_branch_classifier.py`: pre-classify `None` baseline; frozen-replace input non-mutation; per-signal assertions for throw / System.exit / killProcess / setResult / startActivity / finish-without-setResult; setResult-suppresses-finish (no "finish without setResult" reason in the verdict's reasons list); string-keyword `rooted` (suffix-aware) / `PREMIUM` (case-insensitive) / `unauthorised` (British spelling); `rootView` substring excluded by word-boundary contract; length-ratio yields weak DENY 0.45 explicitly *below* `LLM_RECLASSIFY_THRESHOLD`; symmetric-no-signals NEUTRAL/NEUTRAL/0.0; packed-switch with throw/setResult/neutral cases + neutral default classifies each independently with confidence 1.0 from case 0; clean DENY/ALLOW two-branch split; 4-tier confidence pinning test; `BranchOutcome` JSON round-trip via `asdict` + `dumps`. New fixture `tests/fixtures/trace_smali/smali/com/trace/Outcomes.smali` (15 methods + constructor, hand-crafted for deterministic per-method assertions). One pre-existing 10.1 assertion loosened (`summary.switches == 2 → >= 2`) since the new fixture introduces a third packed-switch — original intent was "at least the packed + sparse from SwitchCases". Full suite **649 / 649 green** (was 630; +19). No FE / schema / new-deps changes. **Out of scope (per DEC-024 v1):** cross-method analysis, inter-procedural string flow (only immediate-block `const-string` literals matched), locale-aware string matching (English-only regex; localised gates fall to LLM re-classification), `setResult(RESULT_OK)` vs `RESULT_CANCELED` discrimination. | 10.2 |

**Brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint at the top of 10.6** to confirm the `BehaviorAnchor` JSON wire shape (field names, type discrimination on `predicate_origin`, optional vs. required keys, version field) before 10.7 starts depending on it. Mirrors the 4.1 SQLite-schema checkpoint that locked the call-graph store before the routes API.

**Risks (per DEC-024):**
- Static-only mode (LLM unavailable): the deterministic stack (decisions + slicing + classifier + planner) ships fully usable without any LLM call — `confidence < 0.6` gates simply stay flagged as "needs review" rather than getting LLM re-classification, and the deterministic planner's bypass plans are still emitted. Trace mode degrades gracefully when Ollama is offline.
- Closure walks on dense apps: hard cap `MAX_TRACE_METHODS = 30` (in code, not config) prevents pathological closures; UI surfaces a "trace truncated, narrow the entry method" affordance when the cap is hit.
- LLM-proposed bypass plans are still template-bound (no free-form JS in v1 per DEC-024); the LLM's job is *interpretation + plan selection*, not script authorship. Free-form JS for bypasses lives in `generate_frida_hook` (DEC-022's confirmation gate) and stays as the operator-driven path.
- Intra-procedural slicing only (per DEC-024); `predicate_origin: None` is honestly surfaced and the UI shows a "trace may be incomplete" banner rather than silently fudging the answer.

---

**§ Phase 11 — Behavior Trace v2 — sub-step backlog** *(strictly linear; one sub-step per Agent-mode session; locked in **DEC-025** — operator-UX polish bundle (Tier 1 a+b+c) + bounded inter-procedural slicer (Tier 2 d / closes ISSUE-013))*

Phase 11 ships the v2 follow-up to Phase 10's Behavior Trace (DEC-024). The scope is operator-feedback-driven from the first week of v1 use — three concrete UX deliverables (decision-timeline clarity polish, "Trace from here" cross-tab button on call-graph nodes, `BehaviorAnchor`-aware overlay on the Manual Hooks Cytoscape pane) plus the largest known precision gap in v1's planner (ISSUE-013 — bounded inter-procedural backward slicing for `predicate_origin` via depth-2 descent + type-driven `is_stateless` analyzer + same-class field-write-site walking). Schema bump on `trace.sqlite` (`SCHEMA_VERSION` 1 → 2) wipes interpretation-stale v1 cached anchors via the existing reader's "drop-the-cache" path; LLM budget bumps (`num_ctx: 16384` + `num_predict: 12288`) cover the ~2× input prompt growth + ~1.5× output growth from the deeper `predicate_origin` chains. **Explicitly NOT in v2 per DEC-025:** ISSUE-014 (per-overload precision on two-register comparisons — schema bump on `DecisionPoint.predicate_origin` to a tuple), auto-verify bypass after injection, free-form LLM JS for bypass plans, per-app TTL on cached anchors, iOS / WebView / native-binary `BehaviorAnchor` adapters. See **DEC-025** for the scope rationale, the four pre-answered planning-checkpoint questions, and the v3 deferral list.

The descended `PredicateOrigin` *replaces* the original `MethodCallOrigin` in the slicer's output (operator sees the new terminal, not the chain that produced it — depth pill UI surfaces the "via N helper method(s)" signal per DEC-025 open question 4). Visited set keyed on `(class_smali, method_name, descriptor)` to terminate cycles. (2) **`_DescentBudget`** dataclass shared between method descent and 11.5's field-write-site walking: `(remaining_depth: int, visited: set[tuple[str, str, str]])`. Both passes draw from the same budget so an aggressive method descent doesn't exhaust budget for a subsequent field-write-site walk (closed economy per DEC-025). (3) **`is_stateless(method, classes_by_smali, visited) -> bool`** — type-driven analyzer (new function, ~120 LOC). Walks the method's body looking for *side effects*: `iput-*` / `sput-*` / `aput-*` (field / array writes — definitely stateful), `invoke-*` to non-stateless callees (recursive — reuses the same visited set to terminate cycles), `monitor-*` (synchronization — defensive: assume stateful), exception throws (`throw vN`), and reflection (any callsite the call graph flagged `may_have_unresolved_reflection`). Pure-arithmetic / pure-comparison / `move-*` / `return-*` / `nop` are stateless. New `_STATELESS_LIB_DENYLIST` constant in `slicing.py` listing stdlib classes we *can't* walk into (no Smali source for them, so we have to assume statelessness from the class name): `Ljava/lang/Math;`, primitive boxing classes (`Ljava/lang/Integer;`, `Ljava/lang/Long;`, `Ljava/lang/Boolean;`, `Ljava/lang/Float;`, `Ljava/lang/Double;`, `Ljava/lang/Byte;`, `Ljava/lang/Short;`, `Ljava/lang/Character;`), `Ljava/lang/String;` getter methods (length, charAt, substring, equals, hashCode, isEmpty, indexOf — explicitly listed; `concat` / `replace` etc. NOT included since they allocate), `Lkotlin/jvm/internal/Intrinsics;`. Hand-curated, small, easy to audit, lives next to `_DescentBudget`. **Brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint at the top of 11.4** to confirm the `_DescentBudget` shape and the `_STATELESS_LIB_DENYLIST` initial seed before code lands (mirrors the 10.6 `BehaviorAnchor` JSON wire-shape checkpoint; one-tool-call sanity check on the worst-case fixture cost so we don't ship a slicer that quadratics on a hub-method like `Log.d`). Tests in `tests/test_decisions_slicing.py` (+~18–20 — single-hop descent into helper-method getter; two-hop descent into builder chain; deny-list short-circuits Math.abs; cycle-termination via visited set; cross-class descent allowed when both in same APK; external callee terminates as before; depth cap respected; `is_stateless` returns False on iput / sput / aput / invoke-virtual-with-stateful-callee / monitor-enter / throw / reflection-flagged-method; `is_stateless` returns True on pure-arithmetic getter / `String.length()`-only chain / primitive-boxing roundtrip; visited-set cycle termination on mutual recursion). New fixture file `tests/fixtures/trace_smali/smali/com/trace/Helpers.smali` (~10 methods exercising every descent + statelessness path). | 11.0 (planning checkpoint); independent of 11.1–11.3 |

The descended `PredicateOrigin` replaces the original `FieldReadOrigin` (same UI density discipline as 11.4's method descent — operator sees the new terminal, depth pill surfaces the "via 1 field write" signal). The shared `_DescentBudget` from 11.4 keeps the closed-economy guarantee — a method that descends 2 hops via callees can't *also* walk a field-write site, and vice versa. Multiple writes pick the most recent (last `iput-*` in source order before the read site); constructor-init writes (in `<init>` / `<clinit>`) are eligible (textbook one-time-init field-cached predicate idiom — exactly the case ISSUE-013 listed as v1's largest false-negative class). Tests (+~8 — single-write field; multiple writes pick most recent; cross-class read terminates as before; constructor-init write site; `<clinit>` write site; budget shared with method descent — 1 hop method + 1 hop field exhausts the v1 default `MAX_SLICE_DEPTH = 2`; budget exhaustion in mid-chain returns the unchanged `FieldReadOrigin`). **Brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint at the top of 11.4** to confirm the `_DescentBudget` shape (single shared budget across method + field descent vs. separate budgets) and the `_STATELESS_LIB_DENYLIST` initial seed (the small hand-curated stdlib classes we can't walk into). Mirrors the 10.6 `BehaviorAnchor` JSON wire-shape checkpoint that locked the data model before the frontend in 10.7 started depending on it.

**Risks (per DEC-025):**
- **Slicer depth blow-up on cyclic call patterns:** mitigated by the visited-set in `_DescentBudget` + hard cap `MAX_SLICE_DEPTH = 4` (config-knobbed default 2). Sanity-checked during 11.4's planning checkpoint with a worst-case fixture (mutual-recursion + hub-method patterns).
- **Per-anchor LLM token budget squeeze from deeper chains:** addressed by `num_ctx: 16384` + `num_predict: 12288` bumps in 11.6; if the qwen3.5:35b runtime can't actually use the larger context (some local-LLM setups silently fall back to the model's smaller training-time context), 11.6 falls back to tightening the per-decision prose payload (the `_format_decision_for_llm` helper in `trace_behavior` skill).
- **Cached-anchor staleness misleads operators on first 11.x deploy:** addressed by the `trace.sqlite` `SCHEMA_VERSION` 1 → 2 bump in 11.6 — all v1 cached anchors silently re-build on first open via the existing reader's "drop-the-cache" path. No operator action required, no UI banner needed.
- **ISSUE-013 only partially closes on real apps:** acceptable outcome per DEC-025 — the issue stays Open with a "partial v2 progress" status note describing the remaining gap (likely: multi-step-builder predicates needing depth > 2). Better than declaring victory before measuring on the dogfood app.
- **Type-driven `is_stateless` analyzer adds recursion + cycle detection** to the slicer. Mitigated by the visited-set keyed on `(class_smali, method_name, descriptor)` and the hard depth cap. Worst-case behaviour on a cyclic call pattern: depth cap fires, `is_stateless` returns False (defensive — assume side effects when we can't prove statelessness), descent terminates with the existing terminal `PredicateOrigin` unchanged.
- **Phase 4 CI deferral risk:** if CI.0 doesn't land before 11.4, the slicer changes ship without the regression safety net. Recommendation: prioritise CI.0 sequencing wherever possible; but Phase 11 sub-steps remain individually shippable into a hand-run pytest gate (the same gate every Phase 10 sub-step shipped into).

---

**§ Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up release — sub-step backlog** *(strictly linear; one sub-step per Agent-mode session; ratified in v2.1.0 2026-05-05 — extends DEC-025's "v2 closing note" with a "v2.1 follow-up release ratified 2026-05-05" block + Q7-Q11 lock-in record; no new DEC per Q10: α — still in v2 family)*

The v2.1 follow-up release addresses the operator-friction signal "the Smali entry-method input in Lab → Trace is unintuitive for non-advanced users." Scope locked at v2.1.0 ratification: three coherent deliverables — (1) **Deterministic UX backbone** (`ClassMethodTree` browser embedded in Trace mode + Smali-field collapse + Hops-inline restructure); (2) **Backend coalescer + spinner** (new `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/normalise-entry` endpoint translating dotted input → Smali class-prefix + validating against the call graph, with frontend debounced 400ms inline spinner + ✓/⚠ pill); (3) **Three-tier LLM integration** (Tier 1 "Find similar classes" on validation failure; Tier 2(a) "Ask AI" button opening chat dock with pre-filled prompt; Tier 3 new `suggest_trace_entry` skill surfaced via chat-widget pattern). **Architectural pattern lock per Q7 (ii):** structured-output extension to skill response schema (optional `widgets[]` block alongside `text`; chat renderer detects and renders specialized React components per `kind`) — first instance of LLM-emitted interactive widgets in chat (DEC-022 cross-link); deliberately extensible so future skills can reuse the same `widgets[]` schema + render seam. **Q8 (a):** auto-fire trace on candidate click in chat-widget (matches Inspect → Trace seed behavior). **Q11 (α):** `androscan/skills/suggest_trace_entry.py` lives next to `trace_behavior.py` (canonical skills directory; no `androscan/llm/skills/` exists in the codebase). **Explicitly NOT in v2.1 (deferred to a future v2.2 / v3 / Phase 12):** native semantic-search textarea inside Trace mode (operator's preferred UX is chat-as-UI per Q3 Tier-3 feedback); LLM-driven Smali-signature autocomplete in the "Advanced" textfield (out of scope — coalescer covers the dotted-input → Smali translation deterministically); class-tree filtering by package-prefix beyond what `ClassMethodTree` already supports; widget kinds beyond `trace_entry_candidate` (ship the pattern with one consumer in v2.1, add more in subsequent releases as demand surfaces).
